# NEW PHYSICS LEDGER — what this programme found that (as far as its checks could tell) nobody had

Created 2026-11-21 at Rakshit's direction ("add that and continue"). Every entry carries its status honestly:
**PUBLISHED** = passed a Gate (independent recomputation + literature novelty check) and is in a public release.
**GATE-QUEUED** = computed internally, gate not yet run — believed new, NOT yet claimed.
**UNDER ATTACK** = live signal, red team running — not a finding yet.
Dead-as-ours results (independent confirmations of others' work) are NOT listed here; they live in the gate records.

13. **The Φ ≠ 0 inertness theorem** (Phase 42, Gate-6 restated): "In F(φ)R scalar-tensor gravity (canonical scalar, potential V(φ)) on a Morris–Thorne throat with regular Φ (Φ, Φ′, Φ″ finite at r₀), the demanded matter pair at the throat is exactly the canonical Φ = 0 pair for any such Φ: ρ_dem(r₀) = F₀β/(8πr₀²) − (1−β)F′₀/(16πr₀) and p_r,dem(r₀) = −F₀/(8πr₀²), because every radial-derivative term in the t–t and r–r field equations carries the factor A = 1 − b/r — including AΦ′₀F′₀ and 2AF₀Φ′₀/r₀ — the AΦ′F′ piece of □F cancels identically against ∇^t∇_tF = AΦ′F′, F″₀ enters neither channel, and Φ′ survives only in the scalar equation of motion via R(r₀) = 2β/r₀² − (1−β)Φ′₀/r₀, moving the EoM flux's magnitude but not its sign on the families tested. 14. **Component-blindness of the sampling bound** (Phase 43, Gate-6 restated): "Let the total stress-energy be realised on a fixed background, along a fixed worldline with a fixed sampler f = g², by a fixed content of mutually non-interacting (free) quantum fields, each admitting a state-independent worldline QEI over Hadamard states (scalar, Dirac, Maxwell, Proca class; non-minimally coupled scalars, classical components and interacting components are outside this statement). Then the smeared total obeys ∫⟨T_tot(u,u)⟩ f dt ≥ −B_tot with **B_tot = Σᵢ Φᵢ[f]**, where each Φᵢ depends only on field i's identity, the sampler, the worldline and the background — never on how the fixed total is attributed among the components. Where the per-field bounds share a common functional up to constants (identical fields; flat-space massless species), B_tot factors as (Σᵢcᵢ)·Φ[f]. Hence a decomposition of a fixed total cannot inflate its applicable bound; the only enlargement is adding fields, worth exactly N× for N identical fields. For the 1 m Krasnikov tube that escape needs **N ≥ 1.06×10⁷²** (L = ε; 1.06×10⁷³ at L = a), which is **2.4 orders** beyond the heuristic cap ℓ²/l_P² = 3.8×10⁶⁹ at ℓ = 1 m and **4.4 orders** beyond 3.8×10⁶⁷ at ℓ = ε = 0.1 m (caps heuristic, not theorem), and at N = 10⁷² the Dvali species scale is **Λ ~ M_P/√N ≈ 1.2×10⁻⁸ eV** (cutoff length ħc/Λ ≈ 16 m), so the escape dissolves the semiclassical frame the bound lives in. In curved backgrounds the statement holds in difference-QEI form (excursion below the summed reference-state value). Reopening condition (unchanged): a theorem-grade interacting-field QEI in 4D whose total bound is decomposition-dependent." v1.9.0.

674. **CYCLE 5: THE OBJECTIVE AND THE FENCES CONFLICT — shallowness and the transport band are the same dial read two ways; the family's depth winner at full fences is A = +0.125** (Phase 267, P267-1): 37 members scanned (20 registered + 17 refinement, bump centre c admitted); 7 pass all fences, 10 shallow the region materially (in-region worst NEC > −0.0034), THE INTERSECTION IS EMPTY. 675. **THE IN-REGION NEC DEPTH IS A LAPSE-CURVATURE READ: margin ≈ ρ_eig + 0.028·N″(site), with N″ carrying 95%+ of the depth — and the named exit is a flattened-top lapse shape** (Phase 267, P267-2): p_min is the shift-axis pressure p_x̂ at both worst sites (the eigenframe is exactly the pose's frame); the jet fit's dominant term at both sites is the single monomial c·N″ (c = +0.027259/+0.029757; 95.5%/93.9% of the depth; held-out errors 2.19×10⁻⁶/4.41×10⁻⁶ vs the 2.5×10⁻⁴ bar), corroborated independently by the exact N″ knockout (+0.00561351/+0.00250524) and the ladder response (∂margin/∂N″ = +0.0273/+0.0299, matching the fit coefficients); scan-wide, depth/N″(site) = 0.0257–0.0317 at eleven members' worst sites. The dial is N″ AT THE WORST SITE — linear in A (halving A halved N″ and the depth), scaled by w through B″_w, weak in c. Unlike the density (which provably carries no N′/N″ — the identity re-verified at both sites), the pressure channel carries its own lapse-curvature dependence; lapse monomials carry 95–97% of the fitted depth. Cycle 5's conflict is this mechanism read through the fences: the width dial that flattens |N″| IS the arrival band's dial, and A is fenced below by the Type-I fraction because the region is lapse-made. NAMED EXIT (cycle 6, Phase 271): a lapse profile shape with B″ ≈ 0 at the region's radius (a flattened-top bump) — outside this family, needs its own registration. Basis: CHECKPOINT267.md + p2672_memo.md.

677. **ONE DIRAC FIELD IS T_req AT ALL FOUR DECIDING SITES SIMULTANEOUSLY — the joint match closes at N = 8 (N = 6 stalls on interference geometry, not DOF)** (Phase 261, P261-1): controls PASS (Phase 257's stored match reproduced at R = 1.7677×10⁻¹⁶; the Leg-3 s² table to ≤4.5×10⁻¹⁶; all four sites verified exactly collinear on one ray, Δr = 0.34/0.39/2.16). The sourcing obstruction thereby migrates from algebra to analysis: the field exists at the points; whether it can hold the shape between them is Item 2's question. Basis: CHECKPOINT261.md + p2611_memo.md.
678. **POINTLIKE ONLY — the joint matches are narrow interference nulls: coherence 0.139 m radially, ~0.006 m transversely, against the shell's own 5.66 m variation scale** (Phase 261, P261-2): continuum control exact (Phase 235's analytic machinery reproduces all three β = 1.1 stored site tensors at Frobenius diff 0.0). Coherence lengths at τ₁ = 2.3978×10⁻⁴: radial 0.13935 m ([12.7634, 12.9028]), transverse ⊥X̂ 0.006711 m, in-plane 0.005351 m; L_req = 5.6575 m (h-converged), so L_coh/L_req = 0.0246. Between the sites the fit fails: R ≤ τ₁ at only 5.6% of 551 radial samples over [11.0, 16.5]; R = 8.03×10⁻⁴ at r = 13.23 on the β = 1.1 continuum, ~4×10⁻³ at r = 12 and 14, largest 7.286×10⁻² at r = 11.0 (~30× |T_req|_F); transverse maxima 0.819/0.535 over ±3 m. Covering the deciding band [12.5, 15.0] would take ~18 independent local fits radially and ~890–1120 spacings per 6 m transversely — A 3D TILING, NOT A FIELD. The implied structure is named: position-dependent envelopes / wavepacket modes of width ~L_coh — the envelope/boundary-value construction on the background, priced, not built. Named cheap intermediate (Phase 272): a segment fit against dense radial targets. Basis: CHECKPOINT261.md + p2612_memo.md.

679. **GATE-28: G27-A's fence (i) is REWORDED — the c_trim uncertainty is now a measured live caveat inside the published claim text, with the linearisation priced and cross-checked** (Gate-28): verdict FENCE REWORDED WITH CORRECTIONS. Phase 270's §5 draft is adopted in substance (structure, interval, both ordering statements, closing sentence all verified by the gate's own arithmetic on the stored refit table — the 60-refit distribution, the cross-product total δc_trim ∈ [−1.158404, +0.440454], exactly 5/60 in band, the SE band exiting both sides independently). Three corrections issued: (1) "an order of magnitude wider than the band" replaced by the priced exits (total interval 7.40× the band's width; excursions 10.28× lo / 4.25× hi the half-widths); (2) the locality caveat quantified inside the fence with a curve-based cross-check beside the linearised range — Gate-27's 0.0289 slope is a two-leg linearisation whose tangent leg steepens monotonically across the stored margin curve (local slopes −9.46 → −27.08); solving L(t) + t²δ = 0 on the stored limits gives the curve-based crossing range [0.642595, 0.690610] vs the linearised [0.644750, 0.690957] — BOTH ordering conclusions survive, the lo-side one strengthened (wound clearance 0.080095 curve-based); the fence quotes both ranges, labelled indicative (spread ≈ ±0.0022), neither as a re-computed bracket; (3) the draft's inward rounding at the lo ends corrected to outward rounding. The "most aggressive window" characterisation is ruled FAIR WITH ITS GLOSS MANDATORY: not misconduct (the window was registered in Phase 248 before fitting, eight phases before any bracket existed; never adjusted against a bracket), not innocuous (the stored coefficient ranks 49th of 60 ascending; all 5 in-band refits in its corner; it is not the set's maximum — all 11 refits above it drop the start point — so "aggressive" is quotable only glossed as maximal window on the driving axis). THE STRONGEST HONEST FORM (gate's ruling): Phase 266's certificate certifies the pose as posed, with the stored coefficient frozen in advance, to hold all seven clauses at the 28 sampled instants of [0, 0.675] and cross first at (0.675, 0.68125]; that bracket lies entirely past the wound pose's Gate-23 bracket, and THE LEAD OVER THE WOUND POSE IS ROBUST TO THE FIT'S ENTIRE MEASURED DEFENSIBLE UNCERTAINTY (crossing ≥ ≈0.6426 under every priced refit, clearing the wound hi end by ≥ ≈0.080); the bracket's exact position and any clamped-vs-unclamped ordering are statements about the stored coefficient, not robust to refit, and are not claimed as such. Publication consequence: the v1.22.0 claim list's lead entry (G27-A) has its fence (i) updated in place to the issued text; the rest of G27-A stands exactly as Gate-27 issued it; no new version. Phase 270's named successor is discharged. Basis: gate28/GATE28.md §4–§5.

680. **THE β = 0.105 ROW FAILS ITS GATE — a densified read finds a converged negative SEC tail in the far exterior, carried by the variant-B background, not by β or the shift** (Phase 251, P251-1): the original fine-set result reproduces exactly (worst margin +2.5682×10⁻⁷ at h = 0.3, +2.5754×10⁻⁷ at h = 0.15; Type-I 1.000; audit4 agrees on all deciding signs) — but the registered densified read finds SEC crossing zero at r ≈ 38.42 and reaching −1.6622×10⁻⁸ at r = 39 (h = 0.3), Richardson-extrapolated −1.5417×10⁻⁸ at the binding point against a measured FD error ≤ 9.05×10⁻¹⁰: CONVERGED, not noise. The negative region runs to the domain edge r = 44, so it is not confined to a named layer and SURVIVES RESTATED does not fire: verdict FAILS (checker 13/16, the three non-passes exactly the densified-positivity, 3×-floor, and confinement checks). Anatomy: the stored β = 0.10, 0.1025, and 0.105 Phase-73 finalists share essentially the same far-exterior SEC tail — the variant-B BACKGROUND TAIL is the carrier (the Gate-7 β = 0.10 row is a different construction and stands; C-O1 reproduced it as legal). Cross-item scope note (on the record): any exact variant-B subject should receive the densified radii-35–44 read before promotion beyond its registered wording. Follow-ups: FU-P2511-1 (tail-gated re-polish with radii 35–44 in the objective), FU-P2511-2 (background-tail repair) — executed as Phase 273. Basis: CHECKPOINT251.md + p2511_memo.md.
681. **THE β = 0.12 DESIGN IS RE-VERIFIED LOCALLY — p1423 is NOT retired, and its conversion spec is now FINAL with measured reference floors** (Phase 251, P251-2): all six stored chain reads reproduce bitwise; the full-volume fine audit gives worst margin +2.554732606341442×10⁻⁷ at h = 0.3 (16.68× its measured FD floor) and +2.5592214834670757×10⁻⁷ at h = 0.15 (11.18× its floor); Type-I 1.000 in every region at both spacings; sampled-null minima positive; audit4 agrees on every deciding sign; q_max = 0.9228926835267703 / 0.9227153768552065, both below the registered 0.99 bar; densified probes clean (ramp radii worst ≈ +1.53×10⁻⁵; inner-shell ≈ +2.67×10⁻⁵). Neither MARGIN THIN nor DESIGN WANTING fired. The conversion spec is FINAL with reference floors fixed to the measured values (h = 0.3: 1.5320468431044103×10⁻⁸; h = 0.15: 2.288455080072876×10⁻⁸). This is only the conversion's local half: "CONVERTED AT 0.12" still requires the registered gate-in-design optimisation (kernel p1423) and home judgement of its harvested design. Basis: CHECKPOINT251.md + p2512_memo.md + p2512_conversion_spec.md.
682. **THE RECORD ATTACK FAILS IN BOTH FAMILIES — and flux overload is the onset killer in Procaccia's anatomy class too, for the finalists produced** (Phase 251, P251-3): all controls pass first (hash gate: five copies of x_slp2.npy match the registered SHA-256; C-P1 Procaccia's 0.1363 reproduced legal, worst +3.4042×10⁻⁷ at h = 0.0375; C-P2 his 0.1364 failure reproduced, exact min-null −1.29382×10⁻⁷ second-order / −1.30124×10⁻⁷ fourth-order; C-O1 our Gate-7 0.10 row legal; C-N1 the Nicolini ℓ = 1 0.10 row legal, worst +2.31557×10⁻⁷). Family (a) (Procaccia anatomy, six registered cells, 23 verdict audits): NO row passes above 0.1363 — the common deciding failure is Type-I fraction 0.81–0.92 with negative shell sampled-null NEC from a flux-overload fringe patch, despite several cells' strongly positive eigenvalue margins (margins alone never decide). Family (b) ((10,30) family + Nicolini ℓ = 1): all five cosine rows and all three registered spline polishes fail, principally on Type-I/flux overload at the inner shell — cosine Type-I falls 0.987 (β = 0.11) → 0.713 (β = 0.15); polishes leave Type-I at 0.962/0.924/0.894 for β = 0.12/0.1375/0.14. The ~3 h bound did not bind (hunt ≈ 2 h 25 min), so no packaged scan spec is owed. SCOPE: a verdict on the Powell finalists produced (most starts wall-clock boxed inside the registered maxfev cap), NOT an impossibility proof for the anatomy class. Standing: Procaccia 0.1363 remains the verified mark; our verified best remains Gate-7's 0.10; no scan signal and no record claim above 0.1363. Basis: CHECKPOINT251.md + p2513_memo.md.

683. **CYCLE 6: THE FLATTENED-TOP EXIT FAILS IN FAMILY — the plateau's own area puts every member out of band, and the one member past the depth bar misses the band by more than cycle 5's closest miss** (Phase 271, P271-1): 17 members scanned under cycle 5's fences/objective/bar verbatim; 0 of 17 pass all fences — EVERY member fails the arrival band (Type-I passes everywhere, 0.1137–0.4179; the budget fails only for A = −0.25). The band failure is the plateau's area: at fixed A the plateau holds B ≡ 1 across its width where the C² bump peaks only at centre, so Δv_eff(lapse) grows — deviations +1.26%…+4.87% (A > 0); even the least-deviating member is out of band, and its depth (−0.0456) is the scan's second-worst. Exactly one member shallows past the material bar — scan-best G-E ((2,2), A = +0.25, c = 15, W = 5, e = 4.0) at in-region worst NEC −0.00256972296166494 (r = 16.876) — and its only violated fence is the band, by +2.62% (cycle 5's closest miss was +1.14%): the shape change made the band conflict WORSE, not better. Its QI R_diff = 1951.0450133620295 is the cheapest of any lapse member read so far (bar member 2104.5629; cycle-5 winner 2400.97; D1 2852.41) — bought with the band. Arms: edge width is the class's depth dial (e = 1.5 → −0.020946 … e = 4.0 → −0.002570, edge |B″| ∝ 1/e²); narrow edges are catastrophic (e = 0.75: −0.0456/−0.0829). Disclosed: Amendment 1 corrected a control comparator written against a 6-dp rounded value (unsatisfiable as written; tolerance/bars/family unchanged; pre-amendment record preserved). Basis: CHECKPOINT271.md + p2711_memo.md.
684. **THE MECHANISM SURVIVES AND RELOCATES — flattening moves the curvature to the edges and the worst site follows it; the depth is still the single c·N″ monomial** (Phase 271, P271-2): all 13 DT members have B″ ≡ 0 across the region span (design target met exactly), yet all 17 polished worst sites classify as EDGE. At the scan-best's site (right edge, r = 16.876; B″ = −0.3605, N″ = −0.0901): p_min is again p_x̂ in an exactly axis-aligned frame; the fit's c·N″ term contributes −0.0030971085807219123 = 120.52% of the depth −0.002570, the excess offset mainly by a positive S″ term (+0.001029, the site sitting further out on the shift profile); coefficient re-priced at the edge (fit c = +0.0344, ladder +0.0360 — ≈15–20% above cycle 5's 0.0273–0.0299) while the raw ratio depth/N″ = 0.028518 sits inside cycle 5's scan-wide band 0.0257–0.0317. Updated mechanism statement (verbatim in CHECKPOINT271.md): a compactly supported C² lapse bump cannot have its curvature removed, only moved (plateau → edges, concentrating as 1/e²) and diluted (wider edges → larger arrival integral); the worst site follows the curvature. NAMED ROUTES (cycle 7, Phase 274): the fixed-transport variant (A rescaled per member to hold Δv_eff(lapse) at the +1% edge — depth at frozen arrival bookkeeping; a registration-level act) and arrival-budgeted shape co-design (Δv_eff ≤ +1% as a pose condition, widest affordable edges, asymmetric edges admitted). Basis: CHECKPOINT271.md + p2712_memo.md.

685. **THE TAIL WAS A BUILDER INCONSISTENCY, AND ITS REPAIR IS FREE — the β = 0.105 row SURVIVES RESTATED on the repaired background (phase grade; gate re-judgement spawned as Gate-29)** (Phase 273, P273-1): control PASS (P251-1's failure reproduced bitwise). Carrier = BACKGROUND (static c₃ min −1.7217×10⁻⁸ at r = 39.375; β→0 intercept c₀ = −1.84325×10⁻⁸; the shift contributes −1.0×10⁻¹⁰, 0.6%). Mechanism: the background's tangential pressure p_t = −4.374×10⁻⁸ at r = 39 is a cancellation residue of O(10⁻⁴) derivative terms that fails to cancel because a and b are not mutually TOV-consistent after the builder's two different Gaussian smoothings (a from m + s_P = 2.32-smoothed P; b from m alone, s_ρ = 4.0) — a builder inconsistency priced at −1.84×10⁻⁸, which is why all three stored finalists share the tail. RB-1 (registered cutoff grid): NO FREE REPAIR (all four candidates fail shell invariance; the price is 100% chain renormalisation; the held (32,2) candidate's deciding read FAILS on a self-introduced defect — its TOV tail anchored at the pre-cut P(32) leaves P ≈ 4.3×10⁻⁷ over zero density, DEC ≈ −4.59×10⁻⁷ at every tail radius). RB-2 (Amendment A1: TOV-consistent pressure rebuild, no cutoff): FREE REPAIR at r_c = 32 — ρ, m, b and the chain bitwise original; P spliced to the builder's own unsmoothed TOV pressure (identity verified bitwise); shell invariance Δ = 0.0 EXACTLY. Amendments A2/A3 each forced by a measured artefact in RB-2's far tail (raw h = 0.3 negatives from r ≈ 40.5 extrapolate on a clean h² ladder to +8.624×10⁻¹⁰; the remaining Type-I failures are single wandering points 12–25× below the subject's own significance threshold); non-rescue verified by the checker under the final rule (the original background still FAILS; RB-1 (32,2) still FAILS). Deciding read on RB-2: fine audit legal at both h (worst +4.798×10⁻⁷/+5.889×10⁻⁷, floor 5.696×10⁻⁸; shell reads at r ≤ 30.75 bitwise original), audit4 sign agreement, q worst 0.9764, extrapolated tail margins all > 0 (min +3.615×10⁻¹¹ at r = 44) — top tier unmet (far-tail margins ≪ 3× floor = 1.709×10⁻⁷), so SURVIVES RESTATED with the far-tail scope limit priced in the wording (verbatim in p2731_memo.md). Basis: CHECKPOINT273.md + p2731_memo.md.
686. **THE RE-POLISH CANNOT HOLD BOTH — structurally: the shift has zero amplitude at the binding radius, so the far-tail tier is a constant of the background, unreachable from the shift side** (Phase 273, P273-2): on the RB-2 background, the tail-gated objective's binding term sits at r = 41.75 where the shift's amplitude is zero — the tail score responds to every shift knob (aᵢ, q, r_m) by ≤ 1.6×10⁻¹³ (noise) while the core can only degrade; the optimiser's nit = 0 stop is verified honest by probe. Best joint read = the start point; the row's deciding read stands at SURVIVES RESTATED. The 0.1025 finalist on RB-2 (read grade) shows the same restated profile (near tail raw-clean +4.662×10⁻⁸; far tail the identical vanishing background tail). The only lever on the far-tail tier is a NEW slower-falloff background family built TOV-consistent from the start (FU-P2731-2/FU-P2732-1 — outside this phase's scope); FU-P2731-1 (cutoff repairs must re-derive P from the cut density) and FU-P2732-2 (tail-gated objectives should bind to the extrapolated read or exclude zero-amplitude radii) logged. P251-1's FAILS is thereby superseded ON THE REPAIRED BACKGROUND at phase grade; Procaccia 0.1363 stands; gate-verified best remains 0.10. Basis: CHECKPOINT273.md + p2732_memo.md.

687. **GATE-29: THE β = 0.105 ROW ON RB-2 SURVIVES RESTATED AT VERDICT GRADE — G29-A is its only quotable form; verified best remains 0.10 and nothing joins a claim list** (Gate-29): every deciding number re-derived by the gate's own arithmetic on the stored rows (no phase driver or checker as evidence). Fine set at FULL strength: worst margin +4.79771591905403×10⁻⁷ (8.42× the FD floor; 8.85× at h = 0.15), Type-I 1.000, audit4 sign-agrees. Tail under the final A3 rule: the gated/extrapolated classification recomputes at all 74 ladder rows (32 gated / 42 extrapolated); gated radii have ZERO failures at both spacings; the A2 Richardson effective margins recompute at max deviation 0.0, minimum +3.614955356411259×10⁻¹¹ at r = 44; all 118 raw-negative cells are FD-covered. Top tier fails by a priced margin: the minimum effective margin is 2.12×10⁻⁴ of the 3×-floor bar (1.7087×10⁻⁷) — 3.67 orders below. IDENTITY (attack 1): the Phase-73 row REPAIRED — ρ, m, b bitwise original; the gate's cross-record check finds RB-2's core sampled-NEC minima bitwise equal to P251-1's stored values at both spacings, while the same instrument priced RB-1's chain change at 4.647×10⁻⁶ (the 0.0 is a measurement, not a blind spot); only P (splice at r_c = 32, max |ΔP| 1.131×10⁻⁷) and a beyond the splice differ — exactly the diagnosed inconsistency, nothing imported; the claim's subject is the pair (stored vector, RB-2 background), and P251-1's FAILS on the original background stands untouched, both carried inside G29-A's wording. AMENDMENTS (attack 2): A2 PRINCIPLED (its forcing artefact is verdict-independent — probe ladder ratios 4.002/4.001 extrapolating to +8.624×10⁻¹⁰, the β = 0 arm agreeing to 1.1×10⁻¹⁴ — and it is the same extrapolation instrument that convicted the original row, applied symmetrically); A3 PRINCIPLED WITH ITS COST IN THE WORDING (named as the amendment closest to the line; stands on: its threshold is the programme's pre-existing significance convention computed from the fine set before any tail read existed — the same convention that first CONCEALED the original failure, not a one-way ratchet; the wandering single-point failures recomputed at scales 12–25× below threshold; non-rescue re-verified on the gate's own arithmetic — the original background re-convicts at Richardson −1.5416748387590518×10⁻⁸ and RB-1 (32,2) at −4.593662176670838×10⁻⁷; cost fenced: far-tail Type-I is an extrapolated-class statement, not a raw read). G29-A adopts Phase 273's wording with four corrections (gate-judged status and the BEST-ordering consequence explicit; the bitwise provenance clause and P251-1's standing carried inside; the shortfall priced as a number, +3.6×10⁻¹¹ = 2.1×10⁻⁴ of the bar; the A3 fence added). Publication consequence: nothing joins a findings claim list, no release opened or amended; novelty NONE. The 0.1025 finalist carries NO verdict from this gate (no full protocol on RB-2). Basis: gate29/GATE29.md §4.

688. **A SUPERPOSITION TRACKS THE SEGMENT — pointlikeness along the ray is RETIRED: fitting to the segment converts the interference nulls into a band-wide track at every rung of the ladder** (Phase 272, P272-1): controls PASS (continuum T_req vs stored site tensors at Frobenius diff 0.0; the persisted Phase 261 winner reproduced to ≤3.7×10⁻¹⁷). Setup: 251-point lattice over r ∈ [12.5, 15.0] (spacing 0.01 ≪ L_coh), 2510 component conditions, ladder N ∈ {8, 12, 16, 24}. EVERY rung tracks at τ₁ = 2.3978×10⁻⁴ with coverage 1.000: max R = 2.227×10⁻⁴ (N = 8, warm start only) / 5.765×10⁻⁵ (N = 12) / 3.730×10⁻⁵ (N = 16, every start) / 2.082×10⁻⁵ (N = 24, 11.5× inside τ₁) — monotone improvement (10.7× across the ladder); neither failure form fires (no width law, no flat floor — tracking succeeds). On the finer 0.002 grid, at every N a SINGLE covered interval spans the full 2.500 m band with fine-grid max = lattice max (no inter-lattice excursions) — against the unfitted Phase 261 field's 0.13935 m coherence at the same τ₁. The N = 24 winner is persisted (phase272/work/p272_seg_bestfit.json); the independent checker (direct bilinear builder, all 251 points) agrees per-point to 3.3×10⁻¹⁷. Disclosure on the face: the solver used tr_solver='lsmr' with an analytic Jacobian validated in-run against central FD (it caught a transposed-index bug during development, before any fit); all reported residuals are exact-path, checker-confirmed. Basis: CHECKPOINT272.md + p272_item1_memo.md.
689. **TRANSVERSE: A REFIT TRACKS AT THE SAME N — but the radial winner does not carry sideways, and no joint radial+transverse field is claimed** (Phase 272, P272-2): at r_c = 13.75, two transverse segments (±0.067 m, 67 points each). Read (a): the Item-1 radial winner unrefitted does NOT carry transversely — max R 8.806×10⁻⁴ (perpX, 3.67× τ₁, coverage 0.328) and 3.109×10⁻³ (in-plane, 12.96× τ₁, coverage 0.104); its measured transverse coherence at r_c is 0.04496 m / 0.01448 m against a radial lower bound ≥ 1.25 m (≥ ~28×/~86× thinner). Read (b): an N = 24 refit on the transverse targets tracks BOTH segments from both starts already at stage 1 — best max R = 2.310×10⁻⁶ per segment (104× inside τ₁, coverage 1.000), checker-identical to 4.4×10⁻¹⁷ (persisted: p272_trans_bestfit.json). Stated on the face: the transverse-tracking configuration is a DIFFERENT FIELD than the radial winner — the priced residue is the transverse width, and shell coverage still requires either per-patch fits or the envelope construction; the whole-shell on-background existence question (Phase 253 fence (b)) stands; budget/QI untouched. Named successor (Phase 275): the 2D patch fit — one field over an area, motivated by the cross fit closing at 104× margin from both starts at stage 1 (the open question is area, not axes). Basis: CHECKPOINT272.md + p272_item2_memo.md.

690. **AT FIXED TRANSPORT A FAMILY WINS — the flattened top at A* = 0.0744 reaches depth −0.001387, and the binding fence migrates from the band to Type-I** (Phase 274, P274-1): 14 members, each rescaled so Δv_eff(lapse) = +1% exactly (solver acceptance ≤7.2×10⁻¹⁴); five table-legal members beat the −0.0034 bar. Winner: flattened top ((2,2), c = 15, W = 5, e = 3.0) at A* = 0.07439108177516472 — in-region worst NEC −0.001386956608122417 (r = 12.369, left edge), Type-I 0.1874435411020777, E_neg −2.3079534267714603, QI R_diff = 2529.7054800499386 (the depth win is NOT a QI win — it loses to the bar member's fixed-A 2104.5629 and to every C² row). C² family best: w = 0.75 at A* = 0.1789, depth −0.002366566226251787 — the families differ 41.4% relative at equal transport (the flattened top is genuinely shallower; the shape-independence form is moot). Table structure: depth shallows monotonically as the shape widens, tracking A·B″(site) at the phase-wide ratio 0.021–0.035 (cycles 5–6's mechanism at the new operating point); THE BINDING FENCE AT THE WIDE END IS NOW TYPE-I, NOT THE BAND (the widest rows are shallowest of all — C² w = 1.0: −0.000983; PLAT e = 4.0: −0.000721 — and fail Type-I alone, 0.0659/0.0881; the budget binds nowhere). Cycles 5–6's depth–band trade is replaced at fixed transport by a depth–Type-I trade. HONESTY FENCE (on the phase's own face): every PLAT A* (0.058–0.098) lies BELOW cycle 6's smallest scanned amplitude (0.125) — an unscanned corner; the phase does NOT call the winner a cycles 4–6 fence pass and does not amend cycle 6's grid verdict; promotion is named as a gate question (Gate-30). Basis: CHECKPOINT274.md + p2741_memo.md.
691. **ASYMMETRY IS NOT A DIAL — the worst site follows the narrower edge; co-design confirms the table** (Phase 274, P274-2): at fixed total edge width 6.0 — symmetric (3,3): −0.001387; (2,4): −0.003084 with the worst site on the LEFT (unsoftened) edge; (4,2): −0.003304, site on the RIGHT (narrowed) edge; (1.5,4.5): −0.005064, site on the left edge. Softening only one edge moves the site to the other; the depth follows the narrower edge's A·B″(site) in every row. Best table-legal Item-2 member is 0.001697 DEEPER than Item 1's best. P-WIDE (e = 4.5) posts the phase's shallowest depth (−0.000598) at Type-I 0.0620 — not table-legal. Cross-item consistency: the P-C2 rows reproduce Item 1's C² rows bit-identically. Named follow-ups: the Type-I starvation anatomy at the wide end (amplitude starvation vs coverage/geometry), and a QI-priced fixed-transport variant. Basis: CHECKPOINT274.md + p2742_memo.md.

692. **GATE-30: THE FIXED-TRANSPORT WINNER IS PROMOTED AT THE EDGE READING ONLY — the lapse family's depth winner at full fences, carrying its band-edge fence permanently, and the QI-worst of the fence-passing standings** (Gate-30): the gate's fresh full read at the stored A* reproduces Phase 274's headline set field-for-field (polished depth −0.001386956608122417 at the stored site; Type-I 1245/6642; QI 2529.7054800499386); its own brentq solve returns A* = 0.07439108177509618 (agrees to 6.9×10⁻¹⁴). MEMBERSHIP: YES — the winner's shape dials are verbatim cycle 6's G-E e = 3.0 grid shape; only A differs, and A is a continuous dial of Phase 271's registered class bounded only by pose conditions the member satisfies; cycle 6's grid never sampled A below 0.125; the fences are per-member predicates in every registration, so they apply with no bookkeeping change — Phase 274's rescaling was a search procedure that located the member, not a change to what the fences are. THE BAND EDGE: the registered text is INCLUSIVE (Phase 271 §1's verbatim formula: |v_eff − 1.4736627426242535|/1.4736627426242535 ≤ 0.01; the line's precedent is uniformly ≥/≤) — but the member's deviation is the edge value itself, reached by construction: its stored float deviation is 0.01 + 8.9×10⁻¹⁵, so the machinery's own ≤ flag reads FALSE on the stored read, and the gate's independent solve lands at 0.01 + 3.5×10⁻¹⁶, also False; across all twenty edge members of Phase 274's tables the stored deviations scatter within ±7.1×10⁻¹⁴ of 0.01 and the legacy flag splits 3 True / 17 False — a coin flip of the sixteenth digit, ~3 orders below the v_eff quadrature's registered resolution. The exclusive reading is priced: at A×(1−10⁻⁶) the member is strictly in band (deviation 0.0099999903) with depth −0.0013869552 (a 1.4×10⁻⁹ difference; the depth actually shallows slightly as A falls) — the two readings differ only in whether the promoted amplitude is A* or A*×(1−10⁻⁶); neither permits quoting the member without the edge fence, which G30-A carries in its wording as the only quotable form. STANDING: the winner is the lapse family's DEPTH WINNER AT FULL FENCES — depth −0.001386956608122417 = 36.9% of the cycle-5 depth winner's −0.0037578563113581116 and 20.5% of the bar member's −0.006755961886378213; the first lapse member past the material bar at full fences. The QI ordering stated honestly alongside: bar member 2104.5629 < cycle-5 depth winner 2400.9741802280687 < winner 2529.7054800499386 < D1 2852.41 — the winner is the QI-WORST of the three fence-passing standings (worse even than cycle 6's band-failed scan-best at 1951.0450133620295); depth winner ≠ price winner; no QI standing is re-based. ITEM 1 — DETECTION OF WHAT EXISTS: a decisive retrieval — Zhang et al. (arXiv:1911.10278) Eq. (3.39) gives the vector strains h_X, h_Y ∝ c₁₃, so **ON THE AUDIT'S c₁₃ = 0 SLICE THE MODE'S OWN STRAIN AT ANY METRIC DETECTOR IS EXACTLY ZERO while its energy flux is real**; detection reads are therefore flux + absorbed-power + tensor-equivalent strain (Phase 292's energy-equivalent definition, labelled). HT at 6.4 kpc: F = 4.45×10⁻²² W/m², P_abs ≤ 4.04×10⁻²³ W. At f_orb = 3.584×10⁻⁵ Hz, ALL THREE INSTRUMENT CLASSES ARE OUT OF BAND (PTA above its range; LISA below its 0.1 mHz edge by ×2.8; LIGO ~5.4 orders below 10 Hz) — nature's leg fails on band before amplitude is compared. ITEM 2 — THE ARTIFICIAL EMITTER: a heroic-but-physical rotor (two 10⁴ kg tungsten masses at material-limit tip speed 1.5 km/s, every parameter at a stated limit) has Δs = 1.80×10⁻²⁸ against neutron stars' ~10⁻⁵: **channel dipole power 2.14×10⁻⁶⁷ W; vector-mode power 4.27×10⁻⁶⁸ W** (at c* = 10 the channel power is identical — the flatness echoing in the lab — but the vector share falls to 4.27×10⁻⁷⁰ W). ITEM 3 — THE LINK AT 1 KM: vector flux 3.40×10⁻⁷⁵ W/m², absorbed ≤ 3.09×10⁻⁷⁶ W per test mass at the most generous geometric bound; the tensor-equivalent strain 3.07×10⁻⁵⁷ at 47.7 Hz IS in LIGO's band and fails purely on amplitude — 33.5 orders short (~30 after a year of coherent integration). CLOSING SENTENCE AS REGISTERED: no leg within ten orders of feasibility; **the binding leg is the emitter — laboratory sensitivities suppress the source by (Δs)², ~46 orders against the neutron-star systems, before propagation or detection losses.** The answer to the user's question, in the memo's words: in principle yes (a real propagating mode, universal coupling; bits are modulation, not power); in practice no — the only emitters that exist radiate at a frequency no instrument covers and cannot be modulated by us, and a human-built emitter is ~46 orders short at the source because lab masses are too weakly self-gravitating to couple to the mode. THE CHANNEL IS OPEN IN THE THEORY AND CLOSED AT BOTH ENDS IN THE WORLD. DESK: NO DATA CONTACT. Basis: CHECKPOINT305.md + p305_memo.md.

764. **THE POSITIVE-DENSITY SEARCH IN THE LAPSE FAMILY: NO SUCH MEMBER — AND THE NEGATIVITY IS AN IDENTITY OF THE WHOLE CLASS** (Phase 295): 72 distinct lapse-family members (94 stored rows, duplicates merged) re-read under one uniform protocol; controls diff 0.0 throughout (the winner's re-read reproduces Gate-30's headline set exactly; an independent checker reproduces depth and every site ρ_req at diff 0.0 in a fresh process). 22 members pass the depth bar (−0.0034); their minimum ρ_req spans [−2.795×10⁻⁴, −2.123×10⁻⁵] — ALL NEGATIVE. No member has ρ_req ≥ 0 at even ONE binding site (the family's closest single-site value is −1.02×10⁻¹⁵, a numerical zero). Depth and density are DECOUPLED: the three shallowest members carry among the most negative densities in the family; shallowing buys nothing. THE MECHANISM, EXACT: the winner's ρ_req factorises (agreement ≤ 5.3×10⁻¹⁹) as **ρ = −β²·S′²·(1 − n_x̂²)/(32π·α²)** — a negated square carried by the SHIFT sector alone (the transverse gradient of the shift in the Hamiltonian constraint); the shift-only density is already negative at all four binding sites; the lapse enters only through the positive rescale [α(S,1)/α(S,N)]² ∈ [0.877, 0.934], shrinking |ρ| by 7–12% and structurally unable to touch the sign (N′/N″ appear nowhere in ρ — Phase 258's identity). CLASS-WIDE: ρ_euler ≤ 0 pointwise for the entire two-function (shift + lapse) class, vanishing only where S′ = 0 or on the shift axis (ADM audit family-wide: 288 binding sites, max diff 2.3×10⁻¹⁷). CONSEQUENCES: Phase 293's negative-density verdict was a property of the target's algebra, not of the spinor supplier and not of the flattened-top choice (the profile only relocates sites and rescales |ρ|); the Gate-30 winner joins D1 in the PRICED-NOT-SOURCED class; **the density leg is RETIRED for both drive-line fronts, and the positive-density-target search in the lapse family is CLOSED ON STRUCTURAL EVIDENCE** (an identity, not a scan outcome). REOPENING CONDITION, written at closure per the failure protocol: a drive ansatz OUTSIDE the two-function class in which the Hamiltonian-constraint density is not a negated square — charged as Phase 308 (entry 765). No spinor-match successor triggered (form 1 did not fire). DESK: NO DATA CONTACT. Basis: CHECKPOINT295.md + p295_memo.md.
765. **PHASE 308 CHARGED — BREAK THE NEGATED SQUARE: THE THREE-FUNCTION DRIVE POSE** (Phase 295's registered reopening; the failure protocol's follow-up experiment aimed at the mechanism): Item 1 — ALGEBRA FIRST: extend the drive class by ONE independent metric function beyond (shift, lapse) — the cheapest extension that can break the identity (an independent radial spatial-metric function, posed at the action/metric level with the extension stated exactly) — and derive the required density ρ_req for the extended class: does the negated-square form survive, mutate, or break? Verdict for the item: IDENTITY SURVIVES EXTENDED (the negativity re-derives — the closure extends to the three-function class, and the extension that WOULD break it is named if the derivation exhibits one) / IDENTITY BREAKS (the sign-indefinite terms exhibited explicitly). Item 2 — ONLY IF THE IDENTITY BREAKS: a registered small family in the extended class (the Gate-30 winner's profile carried over, the new function varied over a stated grid), read for depth (Gate-30 SAMPLER-M) and ρ_req at binding sites (Phase 293's site rule): verdicts POSITIVE-DENSITY MEMBER (depth past −0.0034 AND ρ_req ≥ 0 at all binding sites — its spinor match is then charged by the parent) / NO MEMBER AT REGISTERED SCOPE (the depth-vs-min-ρ_req trade curve is the deliverable). The three standing goals ride: the wormhole and record items are the phase's standard riders (any extended-class pose read that touches the throat or shell records is reported; none is presumed). Basis: CHECKPOINT295.md §Item 3 (the factorisation this phase attacks).

766. **THE RED TEAM: CHANNEL BROKEN AT TWO SCOPES — THE FLAT VALUE FALLS BY A THIRD, CORNER B IS EXCLUDED AS DRAWN, AND THE DISTANCE SURVIVES IN SUBSTANCE BUT NOT IN WORDING** (Phase 307, Rakshit's "really try to break it"): controls reproduced every deciding number exactly before any arm ran. **ARM 1 BREAKS (scope: the flat value).** Freire et al. 2012 measure J1738's Ṗ_b^Int/Ṗ_b^GR = 0.9350 ± 0.132 — the decay is centrally 6.5% SLOWER than GR. YBBY Eq. (190)'s tolerance is a linear sum of 1σ-class errors bounding the total ⟨𝒜⟩ − 1, and YBBY never applied it to J1738's Ṗ_b (§VII C.1 sets it aside; J1738 enters their fence via α̂) — Phase 303's fence construction did. At the tolerance's own implicit confidence the DATA-GRADE cap is **0.067, not 0.1** (the 0.1 silently spends the negative central offset as headroom and sits at 1.25σ, defensible at no single stated confidence; at the fence's own 95% convention the cap would be 0.193 and the value would RISE to 5.42×10⁻⁵ — the value is confidence-convention-dependent on its face). The maximised ratio is exactly linear in the cap (verified two ways): **the flat value falls to HT 1.87×10⁻⁵ / NS–BH 1.70×10⁻² (×0.67)**. Flatness itself untouched. **ARM 2 BREAKS (scope: Corner B).** YBBY Fig. 7's bands are EXISTENTIAL PROJECTIONS in the (c₊, c₋) plane (α₁/α₂, masses, P_b, sensitivities varied): outside = clean exclusion. Text-grade digitisation from §VII C.1's own numbers (read-off error ±3×10⁻³; the figure's raster/vector data not extractable — gap on record): the c_V = 1, 2 maximisers hold weakly; the c_V = 10 maximiser is UNRESOLVED on this arm (0.59×10⁻³ past the nominal box edge, inside digitisation error — subsumed by the other breaks); **Corner B (c₋ = 0.16586208) sits outside the combined drawn region by 0.163 in c₋, ~55× the digitisation error — EXCLUDED AT PROJECTION SCOPE.** This contradicts Phase 303 §4(ii)'s "consistent with" recording; WHICH rung-2 modelling choice drives the bands-vs-mechanism-fence discrepancy is a named diagnosis, charged to Gate-33 (entry 767). **G32-E IS NOT QUOTABLE until it lands.** **ARM 4 FENCES (scope: the flat value's pricing framework).** Taherasghari & Will (PRD 108, **124026** (2023) — GATE-32'S CITATION CORRECTED BY THIS ARM — and PRD 112, 024013 (2025)) do not claim the far-zone pricing is wrong; their near-zone dipole (vector-speed only; the maximisers' 𝒞 is 80%/99.8% the disputed scalar piece) would stop the dipole leg binding, kill flatness, and re-price the rows to HT 8.415×10⁻⁶ at c_V = 2 (still above the bar) and **8.42×10⁻⁸ at c_V = 10 (below it)**. Vaglio et al. 2026 engage TW and deliberately adopt the far-zone expression. FENCE HANDED TO THE CLAIM SET: **G32-F stands exactly and only where energy-balance/far-zone pricing is the correct Ṗ_b pricing.** **ARM 3 HOLDS (weak — an absence, worded as such):** the full-EA cosmological dictionary was derived and sympy-verified (G_cos/G_N = (2−c₁₄)/(2+c₁₃+3c₂) — NOT Phase 302's khronometric combination) and no retrievable primary full-EA cosmological constraint excludes any audited point (Zuntz 2008 scans other classes; Audren's EA case imposes α₁ = α₂ = 0 as priors; no EA fσ8 bound exists). Two CONDITIONAL threats against Corner B recorded, neither licensed: the khronometric FB2021 cap transferred (would exclude ×65.8; the flat rows pass it ×119–357 anyway) and Audren's EA-surface c_χ² < 427 limit (Corner B exceeds ×2.47×10⁴ OFF-surface — a method protection, not a physical one; an off-surface EA MCMC is the named instrument that could activate either). **ARM 5 HOLDS:** every published OMW ghost/gradient condition passes at all five points (q_S = 2.5×10⁵–7.5×10⁵ at the maximisers, 24.0 at Corner B; q_V = c₁₄ > 0; all speeds² positive); Corner A's death confirmed a fence death, not a stability death. **ARM 6 HOLDS:** the w₀ ≈ 1 edge is the scalar Čerenkov surface; with it held interior at δ = 10⁻³ the flat value reproduces TO ALL QUOTED DIGITS (degradation only at δ = 10⁻², and even there ×10³–10⁵ above the spin-0 row) — edge-hugging but robust, not an artefact. **ARM 7 NOT APPLICABLE (weak hold):** GW polarisation tests bound detected-signal content, not couplings — four independent applicability failures (the fiducials radiate 5.45 orders below the band; signal content ≠ flux ratio; no EA waveform map exists; the tests assume co-propagating modes). **STATE OF THE CLAIM SET:** G32-A, G32-D untouched; G32-C second-pathed by Phase 306 (entry 762) and not directly attacked; **G32-E BROKEN** (Arm 2, projection scope); **G32-F BROKEN AS VALUED** (data-grade 1.87×10⁻⁵) **and FRAMEWORK-FENCED** (Arm 4); **G32-G (distance-0) NOT BROKEN IN SUBSTANCE** — the bar is met at c_V = 2 under EVERY pricing on record (1.87×10⁻⁵ far-zone data-grade; 8.415×10⁻⁶ under TW) and at c_V = 10 under far-zone pricing only — **but its Gate-32 §5 wording quotes the broken 2.8044×10⁻⁵ and IS NOT QUOTABLE AS WRITTEN.** The affected claims return to Gate-33 (entry 767). Successor diagnoses named by the red team: (i) the bands-vs-fence discrepancy at Corner B → Gate-33; (ii) pixel-grade Fig. 7 digitisation (the c_V = 10 maximiser's Arm-2 read); (iii) an off-surface EA cosmology MCMC. (Aecons)/(Aecons2) — "the only free parameters are then c±" — NOT the full theory. The gate's deciding computation shows that family DEGENERATES at c₊ = 0 identically: its member at (0, 0.16586208) is exactly (c₂, c₁₄) = (0, 0) with α₁ = α₂ = 0 and singular mode speeds, at every c₋ along the line; the drawn combined region is that family's allowed set (extraction confirms its left edge is the family Čerenkov line and its literal c₊ = 0 slice is the origin alone). **Corner B is not that member** — it departs from the family at (α₁, α₂) = (−3.16×10⁻⁸, −3.95×10⁻⁹), inside the Solar-System ranges the bands themselves vary over — and at Corner B's own parameters EVERY constraint YBBY state passes with margins (Eq. (191): |δṖ| ≤ 6.51×10⁻⁹ across all three Ṗ_b systems, margin ≥ 1.54×10⁵ at δ_obs = 10⁻³; Eqs. (194)–(195) pass the §VII B α̂ bounds by ≥ 3.2/5.3 orders — the α̂₂ saturation is against the fence's own tighter L8, not a band leg; full-theory stability passes per Arm 5). Candidate (a) is the presentation mechanism; candidate (b) is EXCLUDED (the σ toggle, anchor vs Gupta PM at ratio 0.9206, moves any Ṗ_b read at B by ≤ ×1.69 against margins ≥ 10⁴, and the bands' σ is a family object never evaluated at B's α's). 774. **THE NEGATED SQUARE WAS THE POSE, NOT THE PHYSICS: THE IDENTITY BREAKS IN THE THREE-FUNCTION CLASS — AND THE FIRST GRID FAILS FOR A GEOMETRIC REASON WITH A NAMED FIX** (Phase 308): controls exact throughout (A1: the M = 1 slice returns Phase 295's factorisation symbolically at diff 0; A2: Cartesian ≡ spherical R, Schwarzschild slice R = 0; A3 ≤ 6×10⁻¹⁹ at the winner's sites; B0: the winner's full read reproduces at diff 0.0 on every field; two defects in the phase's own first attempts were caught by its controls and fixed before any verdict, disclosed in the memo). **ITEM 1 — IDENTITY BREAKS.** With one independent radial spatial-metric function M(r) (γ = M(r)dr² + r²dΩ²; the two-function class recovered at M ≡ 1), the Hamiltonian constraint gives, symbolically: **16π ρ_req = R(γ) + Q_K**, with **R(γ) = (2/r²)(1 − 1/M) + 2M′/(rM²)** — sign-indefinite (the sign of M−1 and of M′ in separate pieces), carrying NO factor of β, S, S′ or N; wherever S′ = 0, ρ_req = R(γ)/16π exactly, of either sign by choice of M. Q_K is the old square rescaled by 1/M plus two sign-indefinite cross terms and one new positive square; R dominates wherever the M-bump is fat or steep (at the winner's sites |Q_K|/16π ~ 4–9×10⁻⁵ against R/16π up to ±10⁻² on the grid). **ITEM 2 — NO MEMBER AT REGISTERED SCOPE.** The winner's (S, N) carried verbatim, M = 1 + m₀·B_plat over m₀ ∈ {−0.75…+1.0}: 8 of 9 members past the material bar; NONE clean at all four of its own binding sites. The failure is EXACT AND GEOMETRIC, not marginal: a single bump's M′ changes sign between its rising and falling edges, the site rule binds on BOTH edges, and each sign of m₀ rescues one edge while poisoning the other. Closest: m₀ = +0.25 flips S1/S3 positive (R-carried, +1.4×10⁻⁴/+1.7×10⁻⁴), failing at S4 (−1.68×10⁻⁴) with S2 at −2.98×10⁻⁶; for m₀ ≥ +0.5 the sites migrate onto the falling edge where R alone decides (≈ −2.2×10⁻⁴…−6.5×10⁻⁴). **ρ_req ≥ 0 IS achieved at individual binding sites in both signs of m₀ — the identity is dead pointwise, not just in principle.** CLASSIFICATION CORRECTION, ON THE RECORD: Phase 295's closure stands INSIDE the two-function pose; its ground is restated — the density leg is a property of the pose γ = δ, NOT of the target's algebra in general. The Gate-30 winner and D1 remain priced-not-sourced, now for a SHAPE reason rather than an algebraic one. NAMED SUCCESSOR DIRECTION (recorded by the phase, charged as Phase 312, entry 775): an M profile keeping R(γ) ≥ |Q_K| at every binding site at once — M′ of ONE SIGN across the binding region, or the bump's edges placed outside the binding radii. No spinor match charged (no member). Riders: no read touches the throat or shell records; Goals 2 and 3 untouched. DESK: NO DATA CONTACT. Basis: CHECKPOINT308.md + p308_memo.md.
775. **PHASE 312 CHARGED — ONE-SIGN M′: THE PROFILE THAT FIGHTS THE IDENTITY ON ITS OWN TERMS** (Phase 308's named successor; the failure protocol's aimed follow-up): the single-bump grid failed because M′ changes sign across the binding region and the site rule binds on both edges. This phase poses M profiles designed against that exact mechanism: (a) ONE-SIGN RAMP — M monotone across the whole binding band (a smooth transition whose M′ keeps one sign at every binding radius, returning to 1 only OUTSIDE the band on both sides, with the return edges placed beyond the outermost binding radius the site rule can select); (b) EDGES-OUT — a bump whose rising and falling edges both sit outside the binding band, so the band sees only the plateau (M′ = 0 in-band, R carried by the (1 − 1/M) term alone). The winner's (S, N) carried verbatim as the base pose, with a registered grid over (plateau height / ramp amplitude, band placement) for each profile class. Reads: depth (Gate-30 SAMPLER-M, uniform protocol) and ρ_req at each member's own binding sites (Phase 293's site rule; SAMPLER-M and exact pointwise both named where they differ). Verdicts: POSITIVE-DENSITY MEMBER (depth past −0.0034 AND ρ_req ≥ 0 at ALL binding sites — the member reported in full; the parent charges its spinor match, and the drive line's density leg reopens as SOLVED AT MEMBER SCOPE) / NO MEMBER AT REGISTERED SCOPE (the deliverable is the trade curve plus the site-migration anatomy: where the sites go as the profile varies, and which term decides at the surviving failures). Controls: the M ≡ 1 member reproduces the Gate-30 winner's read at diff 0.0 class; Phase 308's m₀ = +0.25 member reproduces as the grid's cross-check. Basis: CHECKPOINT308.md (the R/Q_K split and the edge-sign mechanism this phase is designed against).

787. **THE PRESSURE WALL, ANATOMISED: THE VIOLATIONS ARE CARRIED BY THE PROFILES' CURVATURE — S′′ AND N′′ — AND THE M SECTOR IS EXONERATED IN CLOSED FORM** (Phase 316): control passes (the headline member reproduces all six stored Phase 314 reads, max diff 1.9×10⁻⁷ on the sampled DEC margin); the splits are exact (the Einstein tensor is affine in S′′/N′′/M′′; closure ≤ 1.4×10⁻¹⁷, plus sector-off attribution). **NEC at its SET locus (r = 11.8293, transverse, non-Type-I; exact worst-null −1.183691×10⁻³): CARRIED BY S′′** — with all second derivatives off the same contraction is LEGAL at +1.2117×10⁻³; ΔS′′ supplies −2.0440×10⁻³, ×5.82 over the runner-up ΔN′′ (−3.513×10⁻⁴); ΔM′′ ≈ 10⁻¹⁹. **NEC at the polished depth site (Type-I): CARRIED BY N′′** through the minimum eigen-pressure (ΔN′′ −8.2376×10⁻⁴; sector-off N ≡ 1 flips the site to +1.7905×10⁻⁴) — the lapse's curvature IS the depth mechanism, split exactly, so N′′ must be MOVED, not flattened. **WEC (same locus as NEC; deciding observer v = 0.95): CARRIED BY S′′** (ΔS′′ −1.93753×10⁻²) — the boosted contraction amplifies the NEC carrier ~9×, which is why WEC fails deepest at the identical point. **DEC (r = 17.6829): A FLUX FAILURE, NOT A PRESSURE FAILURE** — |T_0i| = 2.4310×10⁻⁴ exceeds ρ = +1.1723×10⁻⁴ there; S′′-carried, necessary and sufficient among the ∂∂ sectors. **THE M SECTOR IS EXONERATED SYMBOLICALLY (verified against the assembly at max diff 2.3×10⁻¹⁹): for γ = M dr² + r²dΩ², 16π p_r = −R1 and 16π p_t = −R2/2 (with 16πρ = R1 + R2) — its pressures are the negatives of its density terms; it is NEC-legal wherever M ≥ 1, M′ ≥ 0, and ΔM′′ ≡ 0 at every locus. THE PRESSURE WALL IS NOT AN M-SECTOR WALL.** THE ATTEMPT: 24 screened members, 5 confirmed; **the density constraint NEVER BINDS anywhere on the surface (ρ min ∈ [+8.9×10⁻⁶, +9.0×10⁻⁵] — the Phase 314 density win is robust under the pressure search)** and every confirmation passes the depth bar; NEC min improves monotonically to the best confirmed member (λ_S = 0.70, m₀ = +6.0, rise [8.5, 19.5]: NEC min −3.639710×10⁻⁴ — a ×3.21 improvement, NOT a crossing; WEC −3.36×10⁻³, DEC −2.89×10⁻⁴). AT THE IMPROVED END THE REFUSAL IS JOINT: ΔS′′ (−2.27×10⁻⁴) and ΔN′′ (−2.59×10⁻⁴) refuse together at a locus migrated onto the M plateau (removing either sector alone still leaves the locus negative; M ≡ 1 collapses to −3.14×10⁻³ — M is load-bearing positive throughout). **ADDITIONAL FREEDOM NAMED BY THE ANATOMY: (S, N) SHAPE freedom — control of S′′/N′′ at the binding radii independently of the profiles' values and first derivatives (an S inflection at the binding radius; N′′ placed for the depth sites and withheld from the NEC sites), with the stated tension that depth is N′′-carried.** Charged as Phase 317 (entry 788). Verdict: NO MEMBER AT REGISTERED SCOPE. DESK: NO DATA CONTACT. Basis: CHECKPOINT316.md + p316_memo.md.
788. **PHASE 317 CHARGED — THE SHAPE SEARCH: SECOND-DERIVATIVE SURGERY AT THE BINDING RADII** (Phase 316's named successor): parametrise corrections to the base profiles that control S′′ and N′′ LOCALLY at the binding radii while holding S, S′, N, N′ fixed there (registered family: compactly supported curvature bumps — stated basis functions, stated radii drawn from Phase 316's binding loci, stated amplitude ranges; the base member is Phase 316's best confirmed member, λ_S = 0.70, m₀ = +6.0, rise [8.5, 19.5], with the headline member as the control base). Objective: maximise the SET NEC minimum. HARD CONSTRAINTS, never traded: SET-244 ρ minimum ≥ 0; depth (SAMPLER-M) past −0.0034; the Phase 314 density win must survive every accepted member. Verdicts: EC MEMBER AT SET GRADE (NEC min ≥ 0 under both constraints — reported in full, WEC/DEC reads stated as they are) / NO MEMBER AT REGISTERED SCOPE (the deliverable is the trade surface over the surgery amplitudes PLUS the deciding question answered: is the joint ΔS′′/ΔN′′ refusal a COUPLING (the same correction that buys NEC at one locus sells it at another, exhibited) or a SEARCH-SCOPE limit (independent improvement still available at the boundary, the direction named)). Controls: both base members reproduce at their stored reads before any surgery read is quoted; a zero-amplitude surgery reproduces its base exactly. Basis: CHECKPOINT316.md (the loci, the carriers, the joint refusal).

789. **PHASE 259 COMPLETE — VERDICT K3: THE DERIVATION SUCCEEDS, THE LITERATURE'S ERRORS ARE PINNED EXACTLY, AND THE IDENTIFICATION STILL FAILS ON ONE NUMBER** (Phase 259, FU-P228-2c): the direct derivation of the semiclassical source tensors, run because both published routes were indicted (Phase 241). CONTROLS ALL PASS (C1: the printed ξ³ coefficient reproduced exactly, the bridge sign fixed on the record; C3: Phase 228's pilot D = (0, +16, −8) reproduced from independent machinery; C2's brief carried one mis-identification — the R□R pairing — corrected by the coordinator on the face of the record, the □-type control passing in substance). **THE DERIVATIONS: H(6,3)(5) = −E[R·Riem²] (63 patterns), H(6,3)(6) = −E[Ric·Riem²] (75 patterns; an 840-matching enumeration proved the genuine Ric·Riem² scalar UNIQUE), H(6,3)(8) in both Riem³ contractions (122 patterns each) — every tensor two-route at diff 0, conservation exactly 0 on generic jets.** ARBITRATION (calibrated decisively: the derived Riem³-chain equals print H(6,3)(7) EXACTLY, so the basis is fixed): **H(5): print − derived is EXACTLY PURE-TRACE at every point — the direct sector agrees; print's error is a trace-sector term only. H(6) and H(8): GENUINELY differ from print, anchored on conservation (print S = +0.4375 / +0.84375 at ℓ=1 vs derived S = 0 — the printed tensors are not conserved, as Phase 241 indicted).** All five derived tensors certified conserved on the full background set by two paths. **THE ASSEMBLY: with the derived (5), (6), (8-B) substituted, the core passes at ξ¹ (3.4×10⁻¹³), ξ², ξ³ (bit-identical to print); per-power conservation S_T ≤ 2×10⁻¹⁵ at ALL powers (print carried +0.030556 / −0.200000); forced deficit, Δρ, Δp_t pass. EVERYTHING FAILING IS ONE NUMBER IN THE ξ⁰ SECTOR: the throat core residual is exactly T^r_r = +16.0 (T^t_t = T^θ_θ = 0), decaying over Ellis R = 1/1.5/2/3 as 16 / 0.291 / 0.053 / 0.0029; Δp_r's excess is exactly that +16 read at ξ = 1/6.** Attribution: the three substitutions overshoot the print residual (−67.556 → +16.0), so the residual ALSO implicates the kept print tensors' ξ⁰ values or the ξ⁰ assembly — not the indicted three alone. **D1 IS CONCLUSIVE: the residual is NOT a reading artefact** (not spanned by the exclusive monomial set under any of seven candidate index readings — exact-rational rank tests fail for all seven) — K4 does not fire. D2 passes on the completed configurations (derived tensors conserved with S = 0 exactly on genuinely non-spherical configurations; the SSS localisation confirmed off SSS). **DISPOSITION PER THE REGISTRATION: K3 — arbitration succeeded but the assembled T_S fails the core and the Ellis gate in the ξ⁰ sector only; NO IDENTIFICATION IS CLAIMED; iteration 2 was not run and is not registrable on this outcome; THE PUBLISHED-DATA ROUTE CLOSES ENTIRELY.** INHERITANCE (FU-P237-1a, charged as Phase 318, entry 790): the failure is compressed from Phase 237's four-power blowup (26.94 / 719.37 / 3955.13 / 7658.62) to a single-power, single-component residual with an exact decay profile; the unarbitrated sectors that could carry it are NAMED: H{2,0}(3) (its variation exists as a Stage 1b byproduct, never arbitrated), H(6,3)(3)/(4), the ξ⁰ assembly weights, and the flagged 263-group trace-reduced scalar. PROGRAMME CONSEQUENCES: the GJW mouth-coupling port (W-ii) remains BLOCKED — its blocker moves from Phase 259 to Phase 318; Row 7's gated cells (K-B via the ANEC-with-coupling read; K-A/K-E/K-G behind it) stay gated; the Phase 297 semiclassical row's status is unchanged in kind (zero evasions, everything gated on an identified ⟨T_ab⟩ — now on the re-solve). Two worker deaths during the phase were infrastructure restarts, recovered from disk with independent re-verification; a flagged D2 completion gap (generic-θ configurations) is recorded as a gap, not a verdict input. DESK: NO DATA CONTACT. Basis: CHECKPOINT259.md + the phase259/ artefact set.
790. **PHASE 318 CHARGED — FU-P237-1a: THE AUGMENTED-BASIS RE-SOLVE** (Phase 259's registered inheritance): the ξ⁰ residual (+16.0 in T^r_r at the throat, exact decay profile 16 / 0.291 / 0.053 / 0.0029 over Ellis R = 1/1.5/2/3) is not a reading artefact (D1) and not carried by the three indicted tensors alone. This phase arbitrates the named unarbitrated sectors in registration order — (i) H{2,0}(3), whose variation already exists as Phase 259's Stage 1b byproduct; (ii) H(6,3)(3) and H(6,3)(4), derived by Phase 259's own two-route machinery if their print forms are to be arbitrated; (iii) the ξ⁰ assembly weights, re-derived from the source's stated assembly rule; (iv) the 263-group trace-reduced scalar from the Stage 2b enumeration — then RE-ASSEMBLES T_S with every arbitrated substitution and re-reads the core and the Ellis gate at all powers. 791. **GATE-35 — THE POINTWISE MEMBER SURVIVES, AND ITS POSITIVITY IS UPGRADED TO A CONTINUUM STATEMENT AT ADVERSARY GRADE** (Gate-35, re-judging Phase 314): controls all pass with the gate's own machinery (fresh split evaluator + symbolic-Einstein assembly, no Phase 308/312/314 imports for any tensor or ρ computation; three independent ρ implementations agree at round-off: C-G1 splits ≤ 1.79×10⁻¹⁵, C-G2 M ≡ 1 slice at ≤ 2.5×10⁻¹⁵ with Type-I count 1245 exact, C-G3 headline SET ρ min at 1.8×10⁻¹⁵). **ITEM 1 — CONTINUUM-CLEAN AT ADVERSARY GRADE:** the angular dependence reduces exactly (ρ depends on direction only through u = n_x̂², affine in u to 2.5×10⁻¹⁶, ρ(u) a ratio of affine functions — the angular minimum is EXACT; the registered 2001-point u-scan still ran at every radius); the radial adversary (Δr = 0.01 over 1001 radii + Δr = 0.001 rim mesh on [19.5, 20], all registered seeds) found **NO NEGATIVE LOCUS — the continuum global minimum is ρ* = +8.100317×10⁻⁵ at r = 20.000000 (the closed outer boundary), u = 0** (R1-carried at +7.9565×10⁻⁵, Q_K exactly 0 there), sitting slightly BELOW the SET minimum (+8.272455×10⁻⁵ at r = 19.878) — Phase 314's rim mechanism pushed to the boundary itself. CERTIFIED CONTINUUM LOWER BOUNDS (found min − margin floor, floors at safety factor 2): inner [10, 12.5] +2.547705×10⁻⁴, mid [12.5, 17.5] +1.167761×10⁻⁴, rim [17.5, 20] +8.081922×10⁻⁵ — found minima exceed floors by ×60/×61/×440, and the floors sit at/below the Phase 312 sibling-pocket depth class, so a pocket of that class could not have hidden between nodes. THE GRADE IS ON THE CLAIM'S FACE: an adversary-grade numerical certificate at this resolution, NOT an analytic proof. **ITEM 2 — THE STORED RECORD IS CONFIRMED, NO DISCREPANCIES** (depth −5.327658635240827×10⁻⁴ vs stored, diff −1.08×10⁻¹⁹, polish point identical; all four binding sites at diffs 0.0 to +9.8×10⁻¹⁹; full SET-244 EC summary at ≤ ~2×10⁻¹⁵ relative; one Item-2 worker died to an infrastructure restart — its code was recovered, audited in full, and every quoted number comes from the replacement's own executed run, disclosed on the record). **ITEM 3 — THE QUOTABLE SET (GATE35.md §5, the only quotable form): G35-A SURVIVES** (existence and stored values, as confirmed); **G35-B SURVIVES RESTATED** (positivity in continuum form at adversary grade, with the grade, the continuum minimum, and the per-region bounds carried in the claim text — Phase 314's set-read form remains quotable as a set read; G35-B is the only continuum form); **G35-C SURVIVES** (depth −5.327659×10⁻⁴, past the −0.0034 bar by ×6.4, quoted as the TARGET's depth — a negative NEC read — never a legality property). THE EC FENCE IS CARRIED VERBATIM IN SUBSTANCE IN EVERY CLAIM: NEC min −1.170007×10⁻³, WEC min −1.044069×10⁻², DEC min −1.000003 (SAMPLER-M) remain violated — a density statement about a target, never a legality or front statement. SCOPE: the headline member only (the other four pointwise members were not attacked; their set reads stand as recorded); posed shell [10, 20], β = 1.1, (S, N) verbatim; no spinor match run or judged. 792. **EXTENT ON THE EXTENDED BACKGROUND: THE BAND FAILS — BY 15% AT THE LADDER TOP, WITH NO SATURATION** (Phase 315): controls pass exactly (the zero-width band machinery reproduces Phase 313's stored point match at max diff 2.4×10⁻¹⁷; CTRL-TGT 0.0). **ITEM 1 — COHERENCE: THE FIELD'S GRIP IS FAR THINNER ON THIS BACKGROUND.** At S1 (τ₁-class measure): radial coherence 0.002149 m (D1 background: 0.13935 m — 65× thinner), transverse 0.001111 m / 0.001926 m (D1: 0.006711 / 0.005351 m). The TARGET varies 3.3× MORE slowly here (L_req = 18.767 m vs 5.6575 m) — the thinness is the FIELD's: its stress slope at S1 is ~0.28 m⁻¹ against the target's 1.6×10⁻⁴. COHERENCE COLLAPSE did NOT fire (the radial scale is 10.7× above the registration addendum's floor) — the band question remained posable. **ITEM 2 — BAND FAILS:** the S1 ray band [11.8293, 17.6829] (5.854 m, 58.5% of the shell support; 251-point lattice + 2001-point fine grid): max residual falls monotonically with N — 1.261×10⁻³ (N=6) → 8.844×10⁻⁴ (12) → 8.359×10⁻⁴ (16) → **3.503×10⁻⁴ at N=24 = 1.15 τ₁ (τ₁ = 3.0463×10⁻⁴), 98.55% fine-grid coverage** — over the bar, narrowly. FAILURE ANATOMY: the tracked intervals are [11.8468, 12.9824] and [13.0527, 17.6829]; the break is a **0.070 m notch at r ≈ 13.0** plus the band's first 0.018 m; only the first third of the band exceeds the bar. **THE OBSTRUCTION IS THE SUPERPOSITION'S REACHABLE SET AT THE LADDER TOP, NOT THE BACKGROUND'S M-STRUCTURE:** the steepest improvement is at the FINAL rung (N=16→24: ×2.39, no saturation), and corr(R, |M′|) = −0.77 — the residual is SMALLEST where |M′| is largest (M helps; it does not hurt). Independent checker agrees ≤ 2.0×10⁻¹⁷. ρ_req ALONG THE BAND: positive at every point of the ray (fine-grid min +5.564×10⁻⁵ at r = 16.4771) — no pocket crossing on this ray; the winner's SET-244 minimum (−7.561×10⁻⁸) lies off-ray, reported as found, no smoothing. DISCLOSURES ON THE RECORD: (i) a FRAME DEFECT in the inherited machinery — eulerian_that's eigendecomposition triad flips discontinuously inside this background's degenerate transverse eigenvalue pair (finite-difference slope ∝ 1/h) — found in Item 1's first pass and corrected by re-posing all continuum reads in the Eulerian frame smoothly continued from S1, via a registration addendum written BEFORE any Item 2 computation; the anchored target reproduces the stored That at S1 to 4.9×10⁻¹⁹; POINT STATEMENTS FROM PHASES 312 AND 313 ARE UNAFFECTED; (ii) the deciding run was killed by a service restart during N=16 and completed from per-start checkpoints with controls and Item 1 re-derived identically. Verdict: BAND FAILS. The aimed successor — extend the ladder past its unsaturated top — is charged as Phase 319 (entry 793). DESK: NO DATA CONTACT. Basis: CHECKPOINT315.md + p315_memo.md.

794. **GATE-34 — ALL FOUR CLAIMS SURVIVE: THE PHASE 312 MEMBER AND ITS PHASE 313 MATCH ARE GATE-GRADE, AND THE SITE RULE IS CERTIFIED ONE NOTCH BEYOND ITS WORDING** (Gate-34, re-judging the 312+313 pair): controls pass with fresh machinery (C1 M ≡ 1 limit vs Phase 295's factorisation 3.2×10⁻¹⁹; C2 FD guard at Richardson-extrapolated 2.7×10⁻⁷, truncation class; C3 split vs stored diff 0.0; C4 the gate's spinor builder reproduces Phase 313's winner at diff 0.0). **ITEM 1 — THE MEMBER, INDEPENDENTLY: NOTHING MOVES** (SET-244 read identical on every statistic — Type-I count 1336 exact, NEC min −0.0031268695933069223, ρ min −7.561242105046518×10⁻⁸; the gate's polish lands on the identical S1; S2/S3/S4 point-for-point; depth −0.0011802747430460965; per-site ρ_req, both NEC reads, Type-I flags, R/Q_K splits identical at ≤ 1×10⁻¹⁷). **ITEM 2 — THE SITE RULE CERTIFIED BEYOND ITS WORDING:** a dense scan (129,042 points) + polishes finds ρ_req < 0 ONLY at r ∈ [10, 10.5] — exactly the strip where M ≡ 1 and Phase 295's identity still governs (6,431 points, |ρ| ≤ 2.49×10⁻⁷; polished volume min −2.552×10⁻⁷ at r = 10.506); SAMPLER-M evaluated on EVERY negative-ρ locus gives threshold hits (a) 0, (b) 0, (c) 0 — **NO BINDING-GRADE ρ < 0 LOCUS EXISTS ANYWHERE IN THE VOLUME at the registered grades**, and the continuum SAMPLER-M polish reaches only −3.071×10⁻³ at a ρ > 0 locus (does not beat S2). Fence 1 STANDS for this member — it is not pointwise-positive (that is the m₀ = +4.00 member's claim, Gate-35) — with the gate's denser volume minimum (−2.55×10⁻⁷) reported beside the SET-244 value (−7.561×10⁻⁸) per the both-values rule. **ITEM 3 — THE MATCH, INDEPENDENTLY: REPRODUCED AND STRUCTURAL.** The gate's own optimiser finds joint fits at max R = 3.0×10⁻¹⁶ (tight tolerances) and 7.1×10⁻¹⁶ (protocol tolerances) — at or below Phase 313's 3.253×10⁻¹³; the N-sweep puts the transition EXACTLY at the counting threshold (7N − 1 ≥ 40 first satisfiable at N = 6; N = 4/5 fail all starts, N = 6 7/8, N = 7/8 7/7); the Jacobian at the gate solution has full row rank 40, σ_min = 0.0272; the perturbation ladder re-achieves the bar at every rung (ε to 1×10⁻⁴) with densities tracking the perturbed targets, positive throughout — **N = 6's success is STRUCTURAL, not a knife-edge fit** (start-success rates are ensemble-dependent and reported as such; the verdict rests on the fits found). **CLAIM SET (GATE34.md §5, the only quotable form): G34-A (member existence/values) SURVIVES · G34-B (binding-site positivity at Item 2's certified scope) SURVIVES · G34-C (match + residuals) SURVIVES · G34-D (density-at-match positivity) SURVIVES** — carrying the three fences verbatim (full-volume, NEC as a negative read, QI absent for the extended class and none quoted). On the record as a rate extrapolation, NOT a claim: a τ₂-class bar at the notch would need ~10⁴ modes by rate alone vs ~180 for the bulk — MODE COUNT IS THE WRONG CURRENCY AT THAT LOCUS (the envelope/wavepacket construction is the named τ₂ route). Correlations unchanged in kind (corr(R, |M′|) = −0.79 — not an M-structure effect). ρ_req along the band positive at every point as found (fine min +5.564×10⁻⁵, no negative interval). ## Publication note (2026-11-24) — erratum to the v1.10.0 release (gate8-claims, G8-2)

The v1.10.0 bundle's G8-2 wording (gate8-claims/RESULTS.md; ledger entry 20) states the P46-3 mirror brane's energy-condition label as "NEC/WEC/DEC saturated-pass" (mirror NEC +0.00072 saturated-pass). Phase 130 (P130-5a) proved the stored K is the + side's: at the mirror [K] = −2K_stored, σ_Lanczos ∈ [−1.472, −1.001], and the mirror's NEC read is **saturated-FAIL −0.0007** in the metric's own convention (two independent paths agreeing to ≤ 3%). The published bundle is an immutable artefact and is not edited — this note is the correction of record for its release text. The completion's formation position (PRE-EXISTING ONLY) rests on grounds independent of the label (Geroch; the constitutive clause; the decision-inclusive inequality) and stands. Record: phase137/CHECKPOINT137.md.

## Standing rule
Nothing moves from GATE-QUEUED to a claim without a Gate. Nothing in UNDER ATTACK is described as found. This ledger is the programme's answer to "what's new" — kept current as phases land and gates run.
